        Laurent Schoelens edited this page <relative-time datetime="2023-08-25T14:30:18Z" class="no-wrap">Aug 25, 2023</relative-time>

    <div id="wiki-content" class="tmp-mt-4">
      <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout Layout--flowRow-until-md Layout--sidebarPosition-end Layout--sidebarPosition-flowRow-end">
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout-main">          <div id="wiki-body" class="gollum-markdown-content">
              <div class="markdown-body">
                <p>Adds deep, reflection-free, runtime-free <code>equals(...)</code> methods to the schema-derived classes.</p>
<p>This plugin also handles <code>JAXBElement</code>s and, arrays and primitive types to generate the best possible <code>equals(...)</code> methods.</p>
<p>Unlike the <a class="internal present" href="/highsource/jaxb-tools/wiki/JAXB2-Equals-Plugin">JAXB2 Equals Plugin</a>, this plugin does not require runtime libraries. But at the same time the generated methods do not accept locators or strategies.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Usage</h2><a id="user-content-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul>
<li>Activate the plugin using the <code>-XsimpleEquals</code> switch.</li>
</ul>
<p>See <a class="internal present" href="/highsource/jaxb-tools/wiki/Using-JAXB2-Basics-Plugins">Using JAXB2 Basics Plugins</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Example</h2><a id="user-content-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example" href="#example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://gist.github.com/highsource/625818b7898dfe4f8ae6">Example of the generated code</a></li>
<li>See the <a class="internal absent" href="/highsource/jaxb-tools/wiki/Sample-Project-po-simple">po-simple sample project</a>
</li>
<li>See the following <a href="https://github.com/highsource/jaxb-tools/tree/master/basics/tests/simple-hashCode-equals-01">test project</a> for usage example.</li>
</ul>
